Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a model algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 737. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 20/3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 737 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the model structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 20. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the model structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 474. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 698 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the model decomposition. By minimality, the element a^474 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 698-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
